# MeridianClothing

Clothing ported from [Meridian Rift](https://github.com/Aphelion-Moon/Meridian-Rift), an SS13 server on the
NovaSector codebase: about 1,900 uniforms, suits, hats, masks, shoes, gloves, glasses and neckwear from
tgstation, NovaSector and Meridian Rift itself. Ids start with `WFMeridianClothing`. Only items Wolfgate did not
already have are here: an item whose worn sprite matches one in the repository, or a single-colour item with the
name of a clothing item here, was left out. Greyscale items that differ only in their default colours are
one item, since recolouring gives the rest; where its name is taken here it is called "recolourable <name>".

About 430 of them are greyscale (GAGS) items in up to nine colours. Their layers are tinted by the `WFGags`
component (the Gags module), and players pick the colours in the loadout window.

Players get the clothes from job loadouts, each department's from its own jobs, and from vending machines:
ClothesMate stocks everyday civilian clothes, AutoDrobe costumes, and each department's Drobe its own.
Civilian loadouts also have the recolourable bunny outfit (two suits, ears and tie), which gives every job's
colours, and AutoDrobe's recolourable items never run out.
Faction uniform groups (TSFMC, USSP, PDV, MMC, Viper Group, ASF) are left alone. Armour, helmets, EVA suits,
gas masks, HUDs and other protective items take the Wolfgate item with that job as their parent and are in no
loadout or vending machine, so where they turn up is for mappers and admins to decide. Adult, admin and antagonist
items are likewise only spawnable.

Species wear the human sprite through their displacement maps (Vox, Resomi, Avali, Harpy, Asakim and the LegStyle
legs). The SS13 species sheets (digitigrade, Vox, Teshari, muzzled, taur) are not ported: they were drawn for
bodies the species here don't have, and a species-specific state would turn its displacement map off.

Everything in the module's `Resources` folders, and its blocks in the loadout groups and vending inventories, is
generated. To change it, edit the generator in `Tools/_WF/MeridianClothing` and run it again from the repository
root against a Meridian Rift checkout:

```
python Tools/_WF/MeridianClothing/port.py --source <Meridian-Rift checkout> --write
python Tools/_WF/Ci/modules.py --write
```

Without `--write` it only reports what it would port; `--report <file>` lists what became of each source type.

- `port.py` names the items, picks their parents, themes, loadout groups and vendors, and writes everything. Its
  header says what the SS13 art becomes here and what is left out.
- `source.py` reads the clothing types and draws their sprites, flattening GAGS configs into colour layers.
- `dedupe.py` compares each item with the worn sprites already in the repository.
- `repo.py` reads the loadouts and vending machines here and writes the marked blocks into them.
